[33m╭─[0m[33m────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[0m[33m Status: PARTIAL_MATCH [0m[33m─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────[0m[33m─╮[0m
[33m│[0m [1mMulti-file context[0m                                                                                                                                                               [33m│[0m
[33m│[0m CancunGasCalculator matches GAS_PER_BLOB=2**17 and TARGET_BLOB_GAS_PER_BLOCK=393216, and ExcessBlobGasCalculator correctly derives current excess from the parent header.        [33m│[0m
[33m│[0m However, AbstractBlockCreator writes header excess_blob_gas using the current block's blobs instead of only the parent's, causing double-counting and invalid blob-fee           [33m│[0m
[33m│[0m progression; the reviewed files also lack explicit max-blob, blob-transaction, KZG point-evaluation, and fake_exponential implementations/checks.                                [33m│[0m
[33m╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯[0m
  [31m● [0m[1;31m[[0m[31mHIGH[0m[1;31m][0m AbstractBlockCreator computes the new header's excessBlobGas from parentHeader excess plus the blob count of transactions selected for the current block [1m([0mnewBlobsCount 
-> [1;35mblobGasCost[0m[1m([0mnewBlobsCount[1m)[0m[1m)[0m. The spec defines header excess_blob_gas as the value prior to the block, derived only from the parent header. This also conflicts internally with 
ExcessBlobGasCalculator.calculateExcessBlobGasForParent, which correctly uses parent blobGasUsed. On the next block, calculateExcessBlobGasForParent would add the same parent blob 
gas again, double-counting it.
  [33m● [0m[1;33m[[0m[33mMEDIUM[0m[1;33m][0m The reviewed files define TARGET_BLOB_GAS_PER_BLOCK and GAS_PER_BLOB but contain no MAX_BLOB_GAS_PER_BLOCK constant or check that the summed blob gas of selected blob 
transactions is <= [1;36m786432[0m before writing blobGasUsed into the header.
  [33m● [0m[1;33m[[0m[33mMEDIUM[0m[1;33m][0m When counting blobs, the code simply maps BLOB transactions to [1;35mgetVersionedHashes[0m[1m([0m[1m)[0m[1;35m.orElseThrow[0m[1m([0m[1m)[0m and sums list sizes. In the reviewed files it does not reject 
zero-blob blob transactions, does not check that every versioned hash starts with VERSIONED_HASH_VERSION_KZG [1m([0m[1;36m0x01[0m[1m)[0m, and does not itself verify max_fee_per_blob_gas >= current blob
gas price or the blob-specific balance component; it only forwards blobGasPrice to BlockTransactionSelector, whose implementation is not among the reviewed files.
  [33m● [0m[1;33m[[0m[33mMEDIUM[0m[1;33m][0m The gas calculator only enables precompiles up to KZG_POINT_EVAL [1m([0m[1;36m0x0A[0m[1m)[0m by passing its last address byte to ShanghaiGasCalculator. [3;35mNone[0m of the reviewed files implement

for z/y against BLS_MODULUS, or returning FIELD_ELEMENTS_PER_BLOB and BLS_MODULUS.
  [32m● [0m[1;32m[[0m[32mLOW[0m[1;32m][0m The block creator delegates blob pricing to [1;35mFeeMarket.blobGasPricePerGas[0m[1m([0m[1;35mcalculateExcessBlobGasForParent[0m[1m([0m[33m...[0m[1m)[0m[1m)[0m, and the parent-excess helper is correct, but the reviewed
